# Legacy → New Parity Audit v2 (2026-05-30)

## Executive summary

The new web app is broadly recognizable but materially behind the legacy product across the board: 40 pages and flows carry 66 P0, 192 P1, and 178 P2 gaps, and only a handful (notifications, account, pnl) score above 65/100. The pattern is consistent — the new build faithfully reproduces the *skeleton* of each surface (titles, tab shells, core chart shapes) while stripping the dense interactivity, full metric coverage, and CRUD/management surfaces that make legacy operationally useful, and in several cases (cohorts, first-product-cascade, distributions, email-sms) the data plane silently ignores filters or returns hardcoded/empty data. This directly substantiates the Founder's four headline complaints: design/UI fidelity has regressed (KPI cards abandon theme tokens and dark mode, Geist fonts dropped, raw-HTML re-skins replace shadcn primitives across the dashboard, LTV, team, account); metrics/numbers are wrong or thinned (paise-vs-rupee COGS money model, stubbed cohorts/cascade math, stripped CM waterfall and ads ROAS/funnel columns, divergent P&L number formatting); onboarding lost its entire "Connect store" step and rich card UI; login/logout/auth diverged visually from the shadcn Card design system; and workspace switching changed model (URL-scoped → session+localStorage with hard reload). Compounding all of this, the entire SUPERADMIN suite (4 cross-workspace admin pages) is completely absent.

## Fix priority order (WAVES)

**Wave 1 — P0 data/feature collapses + the home surface (hardest, highest blast radius).**
- dashboard — the product's home KPI grid lost its 32-metric library, customizer, and date presets; 2 P0.
- meta-ads — read-only shell vs the legacy 3-tab ROAS/funnel/creative workbench; 4 P0, worst ads loss.
- shiprocket — entire shipment-level operational console gone; lowest score (22), 3 P0.
- cohorts — data plane ignores all filters and stubs the matrix math; 3 P0 correctness risk.
- google-ads — generic shared shell, no Google-specific conversion/funnel/creative; 3 P0.
- waterfall — 15-step CM3/Net-Profit waterfall collapsed to a stub; 2 P0 on the core moat metric.

**Wave 2 — Founder-named flows: onboarding, auth, workspace switching, design system.**
- FLOW-onboarding — lost the entire Connect-store step and rich card UI; founder complaint #3.
- FLOW-auth-login-logout — diverged from shadcn Card design; founder complaint #4.
- FLOW-auth-signup-recovery — same design-system abandonment across all five auth pages.
- FLOW-workspace-switching — model changed to session+localStorage with hard reload; complaint #4.
- DESIGN-system — KPI cards drop theme tokens/dark mode + Geist fonts; root of the "looks different" complaint.

**Wave 3 — MISSING admin suite + management/CRUD surfaces that don't exist as features.**
- MISSING-admin-suite — all 4 SUPERADMIN cross-workspace pages absent (score 0); 5 P0.
- settings-integrations — thin 3-connector list vs rich 7-connector branded experience; 5 P0.
- settings-backfill — operator control panel with 7 action triggers reduced to nothing; 4 P0.
- settings-goals — read-only report replaced legacy goal CRUD editor; 4 P0.
- workspace-settings — editable 4-section control panel became a read-only display; 4 P0.
- settings-costs — CRUD cost/expense/salary editor swapped for an unrelated read-only analytic; 3 P0.

**Wave 4 — remaining P0/high-impact analytics + settings CRUD restoration.**
- inventory — 18-column interactive TanStack table stripped to a read-only 6-column shell; 2 P0.
- calendar — lost all marketing-action CRUD, Klaviyo overlay, festival RAG bands; 3 P0.
- settings-ad-campaigns — per-campaign classification editor became a read-only surface; 3 P0.
- pincode-intelligence — 5 columns + key filters dropped; 2 P0.
- customer-lifecycle — dropped legacy controls and gating; 2 P0.
- acquisition — only 1 of 3 legacy sections rendered; 2 P0.

**Wave 5 — P1 metric/feature restoration on the core analytics workbench.**
- analytics — 21-27 KPI card grid + Goal RAG + AI Insight stripped to a subset; 10 P1.
- lifetime-value — raw-HTML light-only rebuild, feature-reduced; 10 P1.
- products — read-only re-skin, lost column customizer + NC/EC columns; 9 P1.
- timings — static-seed rebuild, dropped group-by + columns; 8 P1.
- product-cogs — paise-vs-rupee money model correctness risk; 6 P1.
- store — dropped sync/backfill toolbar, connect-store empty state, COGS surface; 7 P1.

**Wave 6 — P1 sweep on the secondary analytics + settings surfaces.**
- rto-analytics — top-50-product table + empty-state gating + AI sheet missing; 5 P1.
- cod-prepaid — interactive fee-assumption inputs dropped; 4 P1.
- logistics — COD-vs-Prepaid dimension + AI sheet removed; 3 P1.
- first-product-cascade — date/window ignored, primary-product logic wrong; 5 P1.
- email-sms — empty for real local data plane; 1 P0 + 3 P1.
- settings-festivals — read-only rebuild lost full CRUD + shrank dataset; 2 P0.

**Wave 7 — P2 design/behavior polish sweep (highest-scoring, lowest-risk).**
- notifications — not workspace-scoped; otherwise faithful (78); 7 P2.
- account — layout chrome + raw-div divergence; 7 P2.
- pnl — number-formatting divergence vs legacy; 8 P2.
- distributions — hardcoded 3-product seed, but no P0/P1 logged; design-only.
- team — read-only list vs full CRUD team management; 2 P0 + 5 P2.

### settings-goals — parity 28
Legacy: `/Users/rishabhporwal/Desktop/Brain/legacy project/frontend/app/(protected)/w/[slug]/settings/goals/goals-settings-content.tsx` · New: `/Users/rishabhporwal/Desktop/Brain/apps/web/src/interfaces/components/settings/goals-content.tsx`

The new Goals page is a fundamentally different surface than legacy. Legacy is a goal-management CRUD editor (add/update/delete goals across all periods, no date range, no attainment). The new page is a read-only directional-RAG attainment report driven by a date range, with NO way to create, edit, or delete a goal in the UI. They share almost nothing beyond the 11-metric registry and the directional RAG bands. A founder who wants to SET goals cannot do so on the new page at all.

| features | P0 | Entire goal-creation/upsert form is missing in the new page | Legacy renders a full 'Add or update goal' form card: Metric Select (11 options), Period Select (Daily/Weekly (Mon start)/Monthly), Period anchor date input, Goal value number input, Goal type Select (TARGET/MINIMUM/MAXIMUM), and a Save button that POSTs to /api/workspaces/{slug}/goals and upserts the row. | New page has NO add/update form. It only shows a date-range filter and a read-only attainment table. The backend upsertGoal tRPC mutation exists in router.ts but is never called from the web app (grep for upsertGoal in apps/web returns nothing). The page comment itself states 'the inline editor is a follow-up — this slice ships the RAG report view'. | Add the 'Add or update goal' form card (metric/period/anchor-date/value/type selectors + Save) wired to trpc.settings.upsertGoal, MANAGER-gated, generating an idempotency_key UUID per the mutation's input schema. |
| features | P0 | Delete-goal capability is entirely absent (no UI and no backend mutation) | Legacy lists every saved goal in a table with a trash icon per row and a confirm() dialog, calling DELETE /api/workspaces/{slug}/goals?id=… to remove it. | New page has no delete control; there is no deleteGoal tRPC procedure in router.ts at all (only goals query + upsertGoal mutation exist). Goals can never be removed via Brain. | Add a deleteGoal tRPC mutation (MANAGER-gated, RLS-scoped) and a per-row delete button with confirm dialog in the saved-goals table. |
| features | P0 | New page shows attainment for a date range; legacy is a goal manager with no date range | Legacy has NO date-range picker. It is purely a goals registry/editor: list all saved goals (any metric, any period, any start) and create/update/delete them. There is no 'actual' or 'attainment' column on this page — attainment is shown elsewhere (Store Analytics/Acquisition KPI cards). | New page is built around a from/to date range (defaults 2026-05-01..2026-05-31) and shows actual vs goal + attainment% + RAG status for goals matching that range. It does not list goals you cannot currently evaluate, and it cannot manage them. | Restore the legacy management surface (list-all + CRUD) as the primary content; if attainment-by-range is also wanted, add it as a secondary section rather than replacing the editor. |
| metrics | P0 | Goal value unit mismatch: legacy stores/edits decimal numbers; new treats values as scaled bigints | Legacy goalValue is a plain decimal number (parseFloat), e.g. '4.5 for MER', and is rendered raw in the table ({g.goalValue}). No paise scaling — a MER goal of 4.5 is literally 4.5; a revenue goal is entered in rupees as a plain number. | New page treats every value as a scaled integer/bigint: goal_value is z.bigint() in upsertGoal; fmtValue() renders money via formatMoney(bigint,'INR') (paise→₹), ratios via formatBpMultiple(Number(value)) and percents via formatBpPercent (basis points). A MER goal entered as 4.5 in legacy would be interpreted as 4.5 bp by the new formatter, and a revenue goal would be off by 100x (paise vs rupee). Values entered in legacy will NOT round-trip/display correctly. | Decide one canonical storage unit and make the editor + display agree. If keeping bigint/bp scaling server-side, the new form must scale user input (rupees→paise, multiples→bp, %→bp) on write and the legacy plain-number contract must be migrated; verify against the metric-engine Formula Book so MER 4.5 and revenue ₹ values are not 100x/10000x off. |

No Actual/Attainment/Status. | New table columns: Metric, Period, Goal, Actual, Attainment (pct), Status (RagBadge). Set a goal (MANAGER) to track attainment.' The backend listNotifications/getUnreadCount treat workspaceId=null as 'return everything for this user across all workspaces'. | Read the active workspace id (Redux session / route) and pass it to list, unreadCount, and markAllRead so the page mirrors legacy per-workspace scoping (workspace rows + globals). |
